# Skills · C · part 206

761 entries, compaction-handoff to company-knowledge. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **compaction-handoff**: Create a compact, reconstructible checkpoint immediately before context compaction. Persist only durable facts and decisions, overwrite one current resume anchor, verify Git and open-PR state, and emit one concrete next action plus a short /compact line. Use for "컴팩션 준비해줘", "컴팩션하고 이어서 진행할 수 있게 준비해줘", "핸드오프 준비해줘", or "prepare for compaction", or equivalent. Repeated execution must be idempotent and must not grow state files without bound. ([uzysjung/uzys-agent-harness](https://github.com/uzysjung/uzys-agent-harness/tree/HEAD/tests/fixtures/legacy-26.125/skills/compaction-handoff) · ★3)
- **compaction-handoff**: Creates a reconstructible checkpoint before manual or automatic context compaction or a session handoff. Use for "컴팩션 준비해줘", "핸드오프 준비해줘", or prepare-for-compaction requests. Preserves durable facts and decisions, verifies Git/PR state, and overwrites one bounded resume anchor with one concrete next action and a short /compact pointer. Repeated runs are idempotent. ([uzysjung/uzys-agent-harness](https://github.com/uzysjung/uzys-agent-harness/tree/HEAD/templates/skills/compaction-handoff) · ★3)

Load when a last-words context note says so, when preparing for compaction, or when the user says "prepare for compact". ([dereklawhc/last-words](https://github.com/dereklawhc/last-words/tree/HEAD/skills/compaction-handoff) · ★2)
- **compaction-handoff**: Prepare a long-running session for compaction and pick the work up after it — write durable state before the cut, ask for the cut with instructions and a resume prompt, then rebuild from live reads instead of the summary. Use when the user says "prepare for compaction", "let's compact" or "we're getting long", when the compaction mod's prepare note arrives, and before handing the user a /compact to type. ([fairchild/dotclaude](https://github.com/fairchild/dotclaude/tree/HEAD/skills/compaction-handoff) · ★2)
- **compaction-handoff**: Write the one handoff file a compaction would otherwise lose, and pick up from it afterwards. Use when Vlad says "prepare for compaction", "pls prep", "going to compact", "gonna compact", "compacting now, pls prepare", "anything to do before?", or asks you to save state before a /compact, a reboot or a long pause. Also use right after a compaction that brought back a "# Compaction handoff" note. - **compaction-handoff**: Write a durable handoff before Claude Code compacts the context, and restore the working state after compaction. Use it when a [compaction-handoff] or [compaction-recovery] hook message asks for it, or when the user asks to prepare for compaction. Write a COMPACT-FOCUS message only when a [compaction-focus] hook message asks for it. Trigger on any long-context or context-management design.
